# Same topic, different numbers

Generated by build_views.py from CELLS.jsonl (4511 cell records, 196 superseded by a later record, 32 supersession bookkeeping records). Do not edit.

A topic is listed when its records carry more than one distinct `number`. Many are not errors (different population, base, or a later refinement); notes say which.

## CV.H1.uniform

- `C0520` [our workflow: scan-once-wave2, SAMPLED, own_test=True] CV.H1: uniform independent chaining values F_38(IV, M0) (premise H1) -> H1 is used only through the law of x = A_-1 (via the 90 class tests c7_c - x in F7) and, given a pass, the uniformity of the other CV words (Lemma 5.1). Replaced by measurement on 2^36 = 68,719,476,736 real 38-step first blocks (uniform random M0, own GPU code, seed 7): mean passing classes 6.020483 vs exact-under-H1 6.020508 (z -0.62, 95% interval on relative deviation of the good-pair rate +-1.3e-5 = +-1.9e-5 bit); P(some class passes) 0.303193 vs 0.303194 (z -0.84); mean k^2 148.2248 vs 148.2255; pass-count histogram chi2 46.4 on 40 bins vs the exact A.2 table; plain x in F7 4,596,988,233 vs 4,596,957,184 (z +0.47); 256 CV bit means (1/4 subsample, 1.7e10 blocks) max /z/ 2.94, sum z^2 244.0 (expect 256+-22.6); 224 bits of words 1..7 conditional on >=1 passing class (5.2e9 blocks) max /z/ 3.56 (of 224, expected ~3.0); 1024 joint bit counts A_-1 x A-2 and A_-1 x E-1 (4.3e9 blocks each) max /z/ 2.75 / 2.80. No deviation: the exact event with H1 dropped and replaced by the measured law gives the same counts to 2e-5 bit.  (research/dig/incoming2/sha256-38-G04-U05/work/ (h1.cu, build.sh, runall.sh, classes.txt, e)
- `C0521` [our workflow: scan-once-wave2, SAMPLED, own_test=True] CV.H1: uniform independent chaining values F_38(IV, M0) (premise H1) -> None found. Measured 2^32 real 38-step first blocks per family (seed 11): counter in W0 only: mean passing classes 6.020546 (z +0.24), x in F7 z -1.46; counter in W15 only: 6.020418 (z -0.56), z +0.25; W0 = counter with W1..W15 fixed random: 6.020373 (z -0.84), z +0.06; 64-bit counter in W0,W1: 6.020553 (z +0.28), z +0.11; printable-ASCII words: 6.020732 (z +1.39), z -0.28; random weight-4 blocks: 6.019715 (z -4.9) at 2^32 draws but this is duplication (only C(512,4)=2.8e9 blocks exist, drawn with replacement); redone at 2^29 draws with two seeds: 6.020190 (z -0.70) and 6.020512 (z +0.01), bit-mean sum z^2 288 and 299 (expect 256+-23, inflated by the 9% repeated blocks). 37-step (r37) first blocks, uniform, 2^32: 6.020647 (z +0.86). ## CV.diff.nonzero

- `C0432` [our workflow: scan-once-wave2, COMPUTED, own_test=True] CV.diff: characteristics with a nonzero chaining-value difference (first block leaves a near-collision, second block absorbs it) -> Attack uses 1 dCV value (0) of 2^256. Rows 0..3 can absorb (by choosing dW0..dW3) exactly the dCV with the four A words (a,b,c,d = A-1..A-4) equal and the four E words (e,f,g,h) arbitrary: 2^128 values (exact algebra, triangular; numeric: 300,000/300,000 absorbed for E-only deltas, 0/4,800,000 when any A word differs). With the published W16..W19 cells (dW16..19=0 forces dW3=dW2=dW1=dW0=0, triangular) the set is 1 value, delta=0 (0 of 5,100,000 random nonzero deltas leave rows 0..3 identical with dW=0).  (research/dig/incoming2/sha256-37-G04-U05/work/absorb.c, absorb.out, absorb2.c, absorb2.out)
- `C0433` [our workflow: scan-once-wave2, COMPUTED, own_test=True] CV.diff: characteristics with a nonzero chaining-value difference (first block leaves a near-collision, second block absorbs it) -> dCV=0 is necessary for the published characteristic: digest difference = dCV + (A33..A36,E33..E36 difference) and the printed tail rows 25..36 are '='. Tested on real 37-step compressions: published pair + absorbed E-word delta (rows 0..3 identical, W4..W15 published), 1,000,000 trials: digest collisions 0, mean digest XOR popcount 128.0, min 89 (unstructured); control delta=0 reproduces the printed digest. Nonzero dCV needs a tail with nonzero E-diff at rows 33..36 AND zero A-diff there, which needs nonzero A diffs in rows 29..32 (dE_i = dA_{i-4} + A-terms): a different characteristic, not derived.  (research/dig/incoming2/sha256-37-G04-U05/work/absorb.c, absorb.out, absorb2.c, absorb2.out)
- `C0434` [our workflow: scan-once-wave2, LOCAL_ONLY, own_test=True] CV.diff: characteristics with a nonzero chaining-value difference (first block leaves a near-collision, second block absorbs it) -> Pairs following the printed cells of rows 4..23 (traillab SMC, np 24576, 49,131 samples, seed 7002): 6.42% have zero digest difference (3,153; EXACT/cells 1.041 +- 0.007, validates the known 2^-4 tail), the other 93.58% (45,978) have all 8 digest words nonzero, 45,978 distinct 256-bit differences, 0 collisions (support > 2^29.98 by birthday), popcount min 96 median 128 mean 128.0. Shape 'A words zero, E words nonzero' (the only absorbable shape): 0 of 49,131. First pass on 1,981 samples gave the same picture.  (research/dig/incoming2/sha256-37-G04-U05/work/absorb.c, absorb.out, absorb2.c, absorb2.out)
- `C0435` [our workflow: scan-once-wave2, LOCAL_ONLY, own_test=True] CV.diff: characteristics with a nonzero chaining-value difference (first block leaves a near-collision, second block absorbs it) -> Within the published characteristic no alternative exists: (i) E-word deltas (2^128) are absorbed by dW0..dW3 but then need dW16..dW19 != 0 (breaks the '=' W cells of rows 16..19) and a tail E-difference -delta that the characteristic does not have (digest collisions 0 of 1,000,000, min popcount 89); (ii) tail-failure near-collisions are full-width (popcount ~128, 0 of 49,131 A-zero shapes), so a first block would need a 256-bit target. Any real alternative needs a new characteristic with nonzero rows 29..36 AND a new dense part; not attempted.  (research/dig/incoming2/sha256-37-G04-U05/work/absorb.c, absorb.out, absorb2.c, absorb2.out)

## b3.door24.bijection

- `C0290` [our workflow: scan-once-wave2, LOCAL_ONLY, own_test=True] door 24: force more than 4 digest words by construction -> 3 sampled outer steps x 2^22 inner words w0: only Y6 (E1 input) and Y14 (E3 input) vary; #Y6 = #Y14 = #(Y6,Y14) pairs = #distinct w0 (e.g. 4,192,230 of 4,194,304) vs product ~1.7e13; closed form Y6 = ROR7(C2.b1 ^ (C2.c1+Y14)) holds on all 3 x 2^22 points; round-0 state X unchanged (0 of 16 words vary)  (research/dig/incoming2/blake3-r2-G05-U11/work/ (b3own.py, validate_own.py, d21_delta7.py, )
- `C1517` [our workflow: dig-scan-b3r3, LOCAL_ONLY, own_test=True] door 24: force more than 4 digest words by construction -> (A) 3 sampled outer steps (b/flags 55/11, 63/3, 64/11) x 2^22 inner words w0 at ROUNDS=3 (20 points per step cross-checked against verifier _compress at 3 rounds): round-0 state X unchanged (0 of 16 words vary); only Y6 (E1 input) and Y14 (E3 input) vary; #Y6 = #Y14 = #(Y6,Y14) pairs = #distinct w0 (4,192,230 / 4,192,266 / 4,192,253 of 4,194,304) against a product of about 1.757e13; closed form Y6 = ROR7(C2.b1 ^ (C2.c1+Y14)) holds on all 3 x 2^22 points; all 16 state words entering the third round vary with w0. Identical to the r2 result: rounds past 2 do not touch the first two rounds. (B) legal pair A (b55, flags 3) vs B (b63, flags 11), only m4..m7 re-signed so the round-0 state is identical (checked, 16 of 16 words): digest words equal in 100 percent of 2^18 pairs: 8 of 8 at 1 round (known answer), 0 of 8 at 2 rounds, 0 of 8 at 3 rounds; digest bits that never differ: 256, 0, 0 of 256. So the round-0 cancellation alone pins nothing once a second round runs; at 3 rounds no digest word is pinned by construction (word-model X1 for D={4,5} says 8 checks at r3, consistent).  (research/dig/incoming3/blake3-r3-U73/work/d24.py, research/dig/incoming3/blake3-r3-U73/wor)

## blake3-r2 E0 zero-difference relaxation

- `B3R2-E0q1-Q2-scan1` [our workflow: scan-once, LOCAL_ONLY, own_test=True] E0.q1 (a1,d1) (zero-difference relaxation) -> q1 (a1,d1): d1 must be equal (forced by the outputs); a1 is the ONLY E0 intermediate that may differ in an absorbing pair: 2^32 values of da1 free, d(input D)=da1. In-call mass 2^64 per base vs 1 for zero difference (2^64-2^32 with a1'!=a1, 2^32-1 with a1'=a1 where y'=y and dA=-dx, so Y0 must differ); message-only (A,D equal) nontrivial members: 0 (0/60000 random, never 2 outputs)  (research/dig/incoming/blake3-r2-U03/work/b3e0.py; research/dig/incoming/blake3-r2-U03/work)
- `B3R2-E0q1-Q4-scan1` [our workflow: scan-once, LOCAL_ONLY, own_test=True] E0.q1 (a1,d1) (zero-difference relaxation) -> q1 alternatives: a1' = a1 xor dD is unique per XOR difference dD, so the additive pattern (dx,dy)=(d,-d) with dA=0 is unique, and with dA!=0 the split dA+dx=d is 2^32; all 2^64 members give identical (zero) E0 output differences, hence the same later differences; 0 members needing no message difference  (research/dig/incoming/blake3-r2-U03/work/b3e0.py; research/dig/incoming/blake3-r2-U03/work)
- `B3R2-E0q2-Q2-scan1` [our workflow: scan-once, LOCAL_ONLY, own_test=True] E0.q2 (c1,b1) (zero-difference relaxation) -> q2 (c1,b1): forced equal in every absorbing member (B'=B, C'=C in 100000/100000); no relaxation exists in-call, so the zero difference at q2 is necessary exactly. With a message-only difference c1 and b1 differ in 60000/60000 trials (d1 differs)  (research/dig/incoming/blake3-r2-U03/work/b3e0.py; research/dig/incoming/blake3-r2-U03/work)
- `B3R2-E0q2-Q4-scan1` [our workflow: scan-once, LOCAL_ONLY, own_test=True] E0.q2 (c1,b1) (zero-difference relaxation) -> q2 alternatives: none in-call; c1, b1 are equal in all 2^64 absorbing members (B'=B, C'=C), the only alternative route is event-level cancellation against E2, not through q2  (research/dig/incoming/blake3-r2-U03/work/b3e0.py; research/dig/incoming/blake3-r2-U03/work)
- `B3R2-E0q3-Q2-scan1` [our workflow: scan-once, LOCAL_ONLY, own_test=True] E0.q3 (a2,d2) (zero-difference relaxation) -> q3 (a2,d2): forced equal in every absorbing member (y'=y+a1-a1' repairs a2, and d1 equal repairs d2). Message-only difference: a chosen y' can make a2 OR d2 equal, never both (exactly 1 of 4 outputs equal in 60000/60000 targeted trials, 0 in 60000 random)  (research/dig/incoming/blake3-r2-U03/work/b3e0.py; research/dig/incoming/blake3-r2-U03/work)
- `B3R2-E0q3-Q4-scan1` [our workflow: scan-once, LOCAL_ONLY, own_test=True] E0.q3 (a2,d2) (zero-difference relaxation) -> q3 alternatives (event-level): outputs a2->Z0 and d2->Z15 may differ if E2 cancels (c2->Z8, b2->Z7): single-word matches 4021..4204 vs 4096 expected over 2^22 x 2^22 draws, two-word 0; heuristic 2^-32 per base. In-call: 2^64 members reach the same (zero) difference  (research/dig/incoming/blake3-r2-U03/work/b3e0.py; research/dig/incoming/blake3-r2-U03/work)
- `B3R2-E0q4-Q2-scan1` [our workflow: scan-once, LOCAL_ONLY, own_test=True] E0.q4 (c2,b2) (zero-difference relaxation) -> q4 (c2,b2): forced equal in every absorbing member. Message-only difference: a chosen y' makes c2 or b2 equal with exactly 1 of 4 outputs equal in 60000/60000 targeted trials, never 2: each extra equal output costs 32 bits  (research/dig/incoming/blake3-r2-U03/work/b3e0.py; research/dig/incoming/blake3-r2-U03/work)
- `B3R2-E0q4-Q4-scan1` [our workflow: scan-once, LOCAL_ONLY, own_test=True] E0.q4 (c2,b2) (zero-difference relaxation) -> q4 alternatives (event-level): outputs c2->Z10 and b2->Z5 may differ if E2 cancels (a2->Z2, d2->Z13): single-word matches 4021..4204 vs 4096 expected, two-word 0; heuristic 2^-32 per base. In-call: 2^64 members reach the same (zero) difference  (research/dig/incoming/blake3-r2-U03/work/b3e0.py; research/dig/incoming/blake3-r2-U03/work)

- `C0087` [our workflow: scan-once, COMPUTED, own_test=True] message word m13 (w13 = W13 = 0x0007c006; read by D2.a2 in round 0 and C3.a2 in round 1; pinned) -> No alternative outcome: the only w13 with the same d and b outputs for w4=W4 and w4=W4' is W13 (1 of 2^32). Whether a longer message shape (more nonzero bytes in A) could make w14 free with a re-derived inversion was not tested (different length changes the counter word n in the state). (research/dig/incoming/blake3-r2-U13/work/scan_m1415.py; research/dig/incoming/blake3-r2-U1)

- `D243-POSEIDON-D15625-RESULT` [our workflow: dig-poseidon-close, COMPUTED, own_test=True] ALT.FUNCDEC inner degree d=15,625 exact j=0 digit test -> Exact real circuit: nonconst=24 of 26 B-adic digits; decomposes=false; elapsed 104 s. Controls: d=625 nonconst=624/626 and d=3125 nonconst=124/126, both decomposes=false.  (research/sol_answer_D243-poseidon-d15625.md; research/dig/incoming4/work/testD.py; researc)

## poseidon-r2 HSQ rate

- `C1882` [our workflow: dig-scan-poseidon, SAMPLED, own_test=True] PREM.HSQ (PREM) -> r2 pooled 8/23 = 0.348, Wilson 95 percent [0.188, 0.551], 99 percent [0.154, 0.610]; model 0.3935 lies inside. Components: GPU 4/12, CPU 4/11. r1 D189 8/20; r1 per-line 57/180 = 0.317.  (research/dig/incoming4/work/ledger_numbers.json; research/poseidon/d192_r2_results.jsonl; )
- `C1888` [our workflow: dig-scan-poseidon, SAMPLED, own_test=True] PREM.HUNIF (PREM) -> toy p=257 one-centre 0.367-0.404 (earlier work); real r2 pooled 0.348 (n=23) and r1 0.317-0.40 (n=180/20): all within about two standard errors of 0.3935 except r1 per-line (z=-2.11).  (research/dig/incoming4/work/ledger_numbers.json; research/poseidon/orchard/lean_r2/logs/le)

## poseidon-r2 class ceilings

- `PD-r2-ctl-straightline-20261013` [our workflow: poseidon_dig, COMPUTED, own_test=True] MACHINE.CTL straight-line transfer to r2 -> COMPUTED: the transfer is already in exec_r2d (gen_kern.py straight-line kernels, no loop or branch inside a block; ntt_kern.h generated). Remaining control on that build is 19,371,734,050 ops = 38.83% of N; halving it = 0.3114 bit. The gain actually realised by the transfer was not isolated (the old pkg_stage_r2 build had a different attack path).  (research/poseidon/orchard/exec_r2d/gen_kern.py; research/poseidon/orchard/exec_r2d/ntt_ker)
- `PD-r2-class-ceilings-20261013` [our workflow: poseidon_dig, COMPUTED, own_test=True] MACHINE.FMUL/FADD/LDST ceilings on exec_r2d -> COMPUTED from TYPES (N=49,887,124,164): field mul+sqr 12.31% = at most 0.1895 bit if free; add+sub 25.21% = 0.4191; ld+st 23.45% = 0.3855; control 38.83% = 0.7091. The radix-4 route of C1905 can cut only part of the 0.1895; fusing pointwise passes (C1913) only part of the 0.3855.  (research/poseidon/orchard/exec_r2d/logs/exec_run.txt (TYPES line, sums to total_all))

## poseidon-r2 mem peak

- `C1966` [our workflow: dig-poseidon-close, KILLED, own_test=True] MEM.PEAK (MEM) -> KILLED by arithmetic: classical Euclid on two degree-m polys (m=195,312) needs about m^2 = 3.81e10 multiplications (2m^2 = 7.6e10 with adds), vs the 6.863e9-op allowance for lowering D to the C peak (0.186 bit). It is 5.6x over on multiplications alone, each mul charged >= 1 op; current whole stage D is 8.34e9.  (research/poseidon/orchard/exec_r2d/logs/exec_run.txt; research/poseidon/orchard/exec_r2d/d)

- `POSEIDON-R2-TWIDDLE-FORMULA-20261013` [our workflow: poseidon r2 twiddle source audit, LOCAL_ONLY, own_test=True] stage-D NTT twiddle fetch formula -> Generated kernels: normal forward/inverse u_k=2^k-1=(1,3,7,15), peeled b=0 z_k=2^k-k-1=(0,1,4,11). ## sha256-38 memory axis MEM.CODE7

- `sha256-38|MEM.CODE7|MEM|M2` [our workflow: scan-once-wave2, COMPUTED, own_test=True] code of the seven plane programs: 9,946 instructions x 32 B = 318,272 B (lengths 1410, 1413, 1421, 1426, 1425, 1426, 1425; classes per program 16, 16, 16, 14, 12, 8, 8) -> (a) keep ONE program resident (max 1,426 instructions = 45,632 B, saves 272,640 B) and regenerate the program on every class visit: LOWER BOUND 1 op per emitted instruction (mean length 1419.7): dT = +0.088434 bit (T = 87.992169, B = 773,152); (b) same at the package's own generation charge (program generation charged 2^32 ops for the 9,946 instructions = 431,811 ops per instruction): dT = +14.737 bit (T = 102.64); (c) shard by program (drop one program with its classes; ledger with K reduced and P(any class) re-sampled, 6e6 draws): prog0 (16 classes) saves 45120 B, dT +0.0091; prog1 (16 classes) saves 45216 B, dT +0.0088; prog2 (16 classes) saves 45472 B, dT +0.0068; prog3 (14 classes) saves 45632 B, dT +0.0048; prog4 (12 classes) saves 45600 B, dT +0.0046; prog5 (8 classes) saves 45632 B, dT +0.0025; prog6 (8 classes) saves 45600 B, dT +0.0026. The programs are nearly cost-neutral to drop because per-first-block cost scales with the number of classes: the class count only amortises the fixed part (C + 35 + hit list + scalars and planes, about 2% of a first block).  (research/dig/incoming2/sha256-38-G02-U04/work/run_all.py; research/dig/incoming2/sha256-38)
- `sha256-38|MEM.CODE7|MEM|M3` [our workflow: scan-once-wave2, COMPUTED, own_test=True] code of the seven plane programs: 9,946 instructions x 32 B = 318,272 B (lengths 1410, 1413, 1421, 1426, 1425, 1426, 1425; classes per program 16, 16, 16, 14, 12, 8, 8) -> HOLD all seven programs. Every alternative has dS > 0: resident-1 regeneration +0.088434 (1 op/instr lower bound) / +14.737 (package charge); dropping a program costs +0.0025 to +0.0091 bit of time for about 45.6 KB. No byte saving lowers S while B < 2^20. Marginal worth of a program: 0.0025 to 0.0091 bit per 45 KB = 5.5e-8 to 2.0e-7 bit per byte, against the price above the line of 1.3755e-6 bit per byte: each program is worth holding only because the line is not crossed.  (research/dig/incoming2/sha256-38-G02-U04/work/run_all.py; research/dig/incoming2/sha256-38)
- `sha256-38|MEM.CODE7|MEM|M4` [our workflow: scan-once-wave2, COMPUTED, own_test=True] code of the seven plane programs: 9,946 instructions x 32 B = 318,272 B (lengths 1410, 1413, 1421, 1426, 1425, 1426, 1425; classes per program 16, 16, 16, 14, 12, 8, 8) -> Exact: holding all 318,272 B on top of a full 2^20 would cost log2(1 + 318272/2^20) = 0.382421 bit; one program (45,632 B) 0.061456 bit. Shipped B = 1,045,792 is 2,784 B under the line, S = T = 87.903736. Crossing the line by ADDING a program (about 45 KB, 0.0598 bit of memory price) is not worth it: a program carrying 8 to 16 classes is worth 0.0025 to 0.0091 bit of time (7x to 24x too dear). Giving code BACK above the line: dropping program 5 (8 classes, 45,632 B) costs +0.0025 bit of time and would save up to 0.061456 bit if the total were over the line (net +0.0589 there). Regenerating all code at the 1 op/instruction LOWER BOUND (+0.0884 bit) would pay against 0.3824 only if the total were over by the full 318 KB, and loses at the package generation charge (+14.7 bit).  (research/dig/incoming2/sha256-38-G02-U04/work/run_all.py; research/dig/incoming2/sha256-38)

## sha256-38 memory axis MEM.CODEO

- `sha256-38|MEM.CODEO|MEM|M2` [our workflow: scan-once-wave2, COMPUTED, own_test=True] code of the W7 loop and the other routines: 57 instructions (52-instruction tree once + 5 for the loop) = 1,824 B, plus 600 instructions = 19,200 B (the 600 is an allotment, not an instruction list) -> (a) W7 tree not resident, regenerated for each of the 90 tests per first block: LOWER BOUND 52 ops per test (dW7 = +52): dT = +0.049090 bit (T = 87.952826), saves 1,824 B; (b) the 600 other-routine instructions streamed once per first block at 1 op per instruction (dFB = +600, an upper count of what executes per first block, lower-bound price per op): dT = +0.006388 bit, saves 19,200 B; (c) both: dT = +0.055264 bit (B = 1,024,768). At the package generation charge (431,811 ops per instruction) these would be about 2.2e7 per test and 2.6e8 per first block: not worth pricing further.  (research/dig/incoming2/sha256-38-G02-U04/work/run_all.py; research/dig/incoming2/sha256-38)

(research/dig/incoming2/sha256-38-G02-U04/work/run_all.py; research/dig/incoming2/sha256-38)
- `sha256-38|MEM.CODEO|MEM|M4` [our workflow: scan-once-wave2, COMPUTED, own_test=True] code of the W7 loop and the other routines: 57 instructions (52-instruction tree once + 5 for the loop) = 1,824 B, plus 600 instructions = 19,200 B (the 600 is an allotment, not an instruction list) -> Exact: 21,024 B on top of a full 2^20 would cost log2(1 + 21024/2^20) = 0.028640 bit; the 19,200-B routine block alone 0.026178 bit; the 1,824-B W7 loop code 0.002507 bit. Shipped total is under the line (S = T), so no cost now. Above the line only the 600-instruction block could pay to stream (+0.006388 bit at the 1 op/instruction lower bound against 0.026178 saved); the W7 loop code never (+0.049090 vs 0.002507). At the shipped point 2,784 B of headroom remain, absorbing loop-code growth up to 87 instructions (proof Section 14). ## sha256-38 memory axis MEM.CTAB

- `sha256-38|MEM.CTAB|MEM|M2` [our workflow: scan-once-wave2, COMPUTED, own_test=True] class table of the r38 R38M3 line: 90 classes x (c7, base, mask+program number) = 270 words x 32 B = 8,640 B (proof Section 14) -> Ledger arithmetic, each row an alternative (dS = dT because the shipped B stays under 2^20): (a) pack 8 32-bit values per 256-bit word (30 words = 960 B, saves 7,680 B), priced +2.125 ops per class per W7 test and +3 ops per class visit (no variable shifts exist, so a running shift and mask; a count, no instruction list): dT = +0.002232 bit (T = 87.905968); (b) c7 recomputed from base for the 64 classes whose base is itself a member (the 26 added classes: base is not a member; 64 of 90 verified by c7of), 9 counted ops each per first block (+576 ops), saves 2,048 B: dT = +0.006133; (c) whole table regenerated each first block by the exhaustive class scan, LOWER BOUND 2^32 candidates x 9 ops (cheapest member_test path, counted) = 3.9e10 ops per first block: dT = +18.125 bit (T = 106.03); (d) unroll the W7 test per class with c7 as an immediate (-3 ops per class): needs +146,720 B, dT = -0.002884 but B = 1,192,512 (2^20.186) so dS = +0.182689. Holding the table costs no S: the shipped total is 2,784 B under the line.  (research/dig/incoming2/sha256-38-G02-U04/work/run_all.py; research/dig/incoming2/sha256-38)
- `sha256-38|MEM.CTAB|MEM|M3` [our workflow: scan-once-wave2, COMPUTED, own_test=True] class table of the r38 R38M3 line: 90 classes x (c7, base, mask+program number) = 270 words x 32 B = 8,640 B (proof Section 14) -> HOLD all 270 words. S = time_log2 = 87.90374 at B = 1,045,792 (headroom to 2^20: 2,784 B). Every shrink alternative has dS > 0 (pack8 +0.002232, c7-from-base +0.006133, regenerate +18.1); no alternative that saves bytes lowers S because max(0, log2 B - 20) is already 0. The only S-lowering direction would be spending the 2,784 B headroom, and the table has no use for it (unrolling costs +146,720 B, dS = +0.182689).  (research/dig/incoming2/sha256-38-G02-U04/work/run_all.py; research/dig/incoming2/sha256-38)
- `sha256-38|MEM.CTAB|MEM|M4` [our workflow: scan-once-wave2, COMPUTED, own_test=True] class table of the r38 R38M3 line: 90 classes x (c7, base, mask+program number) = 270 words x 32 B = 8,640 B (proof Section 14) -> Exact: S = T + max(0, log2 B - 20); slope above the line 1/(2^20 ln 2) = 1.3755e-6 bit per byte. Shipped B = 1,045,792 < 2^20, S = 87.903736 (no penalty). Holding the 8,640-B table on top of a full 2^20 would cost log2(1 + 8640/2^20) = 0.011839 bit. At the line the packed table (saves 7,680 B = 0.010522 bit, costs +0.002232) would win by 0.0083 bit, but only if the rest of the program were already at or over 2^20; at the shipped point it loses (dS +0.002232). Crossing by unrolling the W7 loop: +146,720 B to 1,192,512 = 2^20.186, S 88.086425 (dS +0.182689): NOT worth it (saves 0.002884 bit of time for 0.18657 bit of memory price).  (research/dig/incoming2/sha256-38-G02-U04/work/run_all.py; research/dig/incoming2/sha256-38)

- (superseded by D628-SHA256-R38-PADDING-Q2-EXACT-CLOSURE-20261014) `C0535` [our workflow: scan-once-wave2, OPEN, own_test=True] padding block as separate block 3 (vs block 2 being the last block) -> NOT COMPLETED. Exact so far (rows <=13 of published chi38 held): dW14=0 is already the published pattern, 160 of 2^32 W14 values pass row 14 (A-cell pass 134,217,728; published W14 d28e48a0 among them). The decisive step (for each of the 160, scan all 2^32 W15 with dW15=0 for row 15) was started but killed unfinished at the time limit (machine load ~29); no result, UNKNOWN not UNSAT. In chi38 only dW15 would have to be removed (3 difference cells n,n,u in W15 and row-15 A/E cells)  (research/dig/incoming2/sha256-38-G04-U08/work/pad.c; work/run.sh; work/out_pub.txt; work/o)
- `G04U08-sha256-38-CV-PADlen-CV-Q2-dw15zero` [our workflow: exact-source-proof, KILLED, own_test=True] padding block as separate block 3: dW15=0 branch -> EXACT UNSAT for the stated branch. chi38 row 15's W word has two n cells and one u cell. The proposed dW15=0 condition makes the two W15 words equal, but an n cell requires (0,1) and a u cell requires (1,0); equality violates both. Thus the row-15 continuation count is exactly zero for every W15 value, and the unfinished 160 x 2^32 scan is unnecessary. This closes only the retained-characteristic dW15=0 branch; a redesigned characteristic/block layout remains OPEN.  (research/retest/sha256_r38_padlen_q2_proof.py; research/tools/traillab/chi/chi38.txt; rese)

## sha3-r6 leader route chi1 conditions

- `C0112` [our workflow: scan-once, COMPUTED, own_test=True] round-1 chi on the leader route (4867f093 prefix conditions, 32 z-directions) -> 128 affine conditions, rank 128, on the message: 4 per z-bit (for each of the 2 chi1-input bits a z-flip moves, its row neighbour x+1 must be 0 and x-1 must be 1). Same 128 in the 64-byte (512 free bits) and 135-byte (1080 free bits) formats; none depends on z (0 of 128x32 form/direction pairs). The leader's own 256-equation prefix (L3=0x06^M64^rotl(L0^L5,1), L4=0, L6=0, L7=L2^rotr(L0^L5,1)) satisfies all 128 (100%, plain lanes), so it over-conditions by 128 on unused planes. Validation (known answer reproduced): on 300 satisfying prefixes x 32 directions every chi1 output difference has weight 2 and equals the input difference (9,600/9,600), theta2 footprint 22 words, 21 chi2 rows, 1 two-input row; unconstrained prefixes: identity in only 5.7% of cases, mean weight 3.99.  (research/dig/incoming/sha3-r6-U01/work/sha3lab.py; research/dig/incoming/sha3-r6-U01/work/)
- `C0113` [our workflow: scan-once, SAMPLED, own_test=True] round-1 chi on the leader route: necessity of each of the 128 conditions -> Affinity and rank never fail: dropping any single one of the 128 conditions (all independent, none redundant), dropping a whole family of 32, or dropping all 128 gives 0 second-difference failures (76,800 checks per format for single drops, 600 per family, 1,000 with none) and the 32 z-images keep rank 32. Reason (structural): each chi1 row holds at most one z-varying bit, so chi1 is affine in z with no condition at all. What each condition buys is constancy/sparsity: dropping one makes that direction's chi1 difference prefix-dependent (2 distinct differences, weight 3 in 32-68% of 60 samples, mean 0.49 F64 / 0.50 F135) and raises the theta2 footprint from 22 to 31 or 33 words and chi2 rows from 21 to 30-32 in the violated cases. Dropping a family of 32: mean weight 2.49; none: 3.99.  (research/dig/incoming/sha3-r6-U01/work/q12.py; research/dig/incoming/sha3-r6-U01/work/q12_)

## sha3-r6 leader route freedom

- `C0114` [our workflow: scan-once, COMPUTED, own_test=True] round-1 freedom on the leader route and the linstruct 24/35-dim spaces -> Leader route (32 z-dims, 128 conditions): 2^352 groups in the 64-byte format (512 bits), 2^920 groups in the 135-byte format (1,080 bits); 2^96 groups x 2^32 are needed for N=2^128, so headroom 256 / 824 bits. Leader's 256-equation prefix: at most 2^224 groups (2^256 prefixes less the L0/L5 z overlap), still 128 bits of headroom. linstruct re-measured with our Keccak: the listed 31-direction set has GF(2) rank 24, 82 conditions rank 82, outer freedom 1080-24-82 = 2^974, second-difference test on the real 2-round function 0/300 fails (controls: conditions ignored 200/200 fail; conditions met but 3 rounds 200/200 fail); SOLCLOUD_16 35-dim space: rank 35, 2^320 source, outer 2^285, 0/300 fails. The other listed 24-direction run has rank 19, 67 conditions, outer 2^994.  (research/dig/incoming/sha3-r6-U01/work/q3.py; research/dig/incoming/sha3-r6-U01/work/q3_ou)
- `C0119` [our workflow: scan-once, COMPUTED, own_test=True] log2 freedom left under the leader's prefix conditions at the chi2 patch -> 1080 message bits - rank 312 = 768 free bits (log2 = 768; free lanes 0,1,2,3,4,6,7,9,10,11,13,14); 32 of them are the z counter (XORed into lane4 and lane14 low 32 bits, column parity cancels), leaving 736 prefix bits; a 2^128 birthday needs 128, slack 640 (608 after z). With only the 128 equations the z-flips need, 952 bits would be free (+184). chi2 itself adds no condition on the free bits: its change is computed from the state (AND-free), so no freedom is spent at chi2.  (research/dig/incoming/sha3-r6-U02/work/t2_conditions.py; research/dig/incoming/sha3-r6-U02)

## sha3r6.ledger.cone5

- `sha3-r6|STAGE.CONE5|STAGE|Q1` [our workflow: scan-once-wave2, COMPUTED, own_test=True] r6 ledger block round-5 cone (175 words, 2.19 ops/message) -> Own count of the 175-word block (= theta6 apply producing the 175 chi6 input words; ops/msg = word ops/256): ALU only 350 = 1.367; +load a, +store T, C in regs 700 = 2.734; C loaded per D (2 loads) 1050 = 4.102; C loaded once per column 1001 = 3.91. Stated 2.19 = 560.6 word ops; NOT reproduced by any model (3 ops/word load-xor-store = 525 plus 1/row = 560 matches the number but is a reading, not a derivation). Column parities for the 301 columns cost 1204 ALU if computed here (4.70 ops/msg), so the leader's block must inherit them from the previous store ('theta parities at store').  (research/dig/incoming2/sha3-r6-G06-U07/work/cnt.py; cnt_out.json; comp.py; comp_out.json; )
- `sha3-r6|STAGE.CONE5|STAGE|Q2` [our workflow: scan-once-wave2, COMPUTED, own_test=True] r6 ledger block round-5 cone (175 words) -> Not droppable: flipping any one of the 175 B words changes some key bit (175 of 175 necessary, 256 random messages each); no D is shared (175 distinct D for 175 words), so no word can be derived from another. Mergeable: fusing it row-wise with the chi6 block keeps the 175 words in registers and removes 175 stores + 175 loads = 350 word ops = 1.367 ops/msg (load/store counting rule; separate 1787 vs fused 1437 ops/step in our model).  (research/dig/incoming2/sha3-r6-G06-U07/work/cnt.py; cnt_out.json; comp.py; comp_out.json; )
